[[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1906978209}[实例视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/IPv4 VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/IPv6 VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1631120862}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x693168895}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1102154159}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x132712900}

[*[vpn-target]{lang="EN-US"}*[&\<1-8\>]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1795044372}[：指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Route Target]{lang="EN-US"}[值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vpn-target]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[21]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，取值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[AS-number:nn]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP-address:nn]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[&\<1-8\>]{lang="EN-US"}[表示前面的参数最多可以输入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[8]{lang="EN-US"}[次。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[Route Target]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_2033096306}[有三种格式：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[16]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1919688548}[位自治系统号]{style="font-family:宋体"}[:32]{lang="EN-US"}[位用户自定义数，例如：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[101:3]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[32]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_499296695}[位]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址]{style="font-family:宋体"}[:16]{lang="EN-US"}[位用户自定义数，例如：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[192.168.122.15:1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[32]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1542138770}[位自治系统号：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[16]{lang="EN-US"}[位用户自定义数字，其中的自治系统号最小值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65536]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[例如：]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[65536:1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[both]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_972941459}[：指定配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Route Target]{lang="EN-US"}[值同时作为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Import Target]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Export Target]{lang="EN-US"}[。没有指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[both]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[export-extcommunity]{lang="EN-US"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[import-extcommunity]{lang="EN-US"}**[中的任何一个参数时，缺省值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[both]{lang="EN-US"}**[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[export]{lang="EN-US"}[-extcommunity]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1102219695}[：指定配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Route Target]{lang="EN-US"}[值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Export Target]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[import-extcommunity]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x2085798506}[：指定配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Route Target]{lang="EN-US"}[值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Import Target]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[all]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x3190422}[：所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Route Target]{lang="EN-US"}[值]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1750600358}

[[Route Target]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1512711371}[用来控制]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[路由的发布。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[在发布的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[路由中添加]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Route Target]{lang="EN-US"}[扩展团体属性，该属性的值为配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Export Target]{lang="EN-US"}[。对端]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[接收到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[路由后，将路由中携带的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Route Target]{lang="EN-US"}[属性与本地配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[实例的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Import Target]{lang="EN-US"}[进行比较，如果二者中存在相同的值，则将该路由学习到该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[实例的路由表中。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_516722721}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1526297414}[实例视图下的配置，既可以用于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4 VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[，也可以用于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6 VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[；]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4 VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[视图下的配置只能用于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4 VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[；]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6 VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[视图下的配置只能用于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6 VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[IPv4 VPN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1789247367}[视图、]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[IPv6 VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[视图下的配置优先级高于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[实例视图下的配置，即如果同时在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4 VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[视图（或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6 VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[视图）和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[实例视图下进行了配置，则]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4 VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[（或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6 VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[）采用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4 VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[视图（或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6 VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[视图）下的配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1188418950}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1884106223}[为名为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[vpn1]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[实例配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Route Target]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1102285231}

[\[Sysname\] ip vpn-instance vpn1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-vpn-instance-vpn1\] vpn-target 3:3 export-extcommunity]{lang="EN-US"}

**IPv6 MPLS L3VPN \-- IPv6 MPLS L3VPN配置命令 \-- address-family vpnv6**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[address-family vpnv6]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1626467049}[命令用来创建]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址族，并进入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址族视图。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo address-family vpnv6]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1134435018}[命令用来删除]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址族及该视图下的所有配置。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1026336043}

[**[address-family vpnv6]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1575412885}

[**[undo address-family vpnv6]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1533452010}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1626401513}

[[没有创建]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x2053739223}[地址族。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1339729455}

[[BGP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x832748292}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1971292055}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x2131419518}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_364931653}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1626335977}

[[VPN-IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x248003928}[地址是指在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址前缀前增加]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RD]{lang="EN-US"}[后，形成的地址。在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6 MPLS L3VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[组网中，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[设备之间需要交换]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN-IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址族的路由信息（即]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1287166929}[设备上，进入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址族视图，在该视图下通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[peer enable]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP]{lang="EN-US"}[对等体（通常为对端]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[设备）后，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[才能与该对等体交换]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x491954041}[地址族视图下，还可以配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP-VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由的属性，例如为从对等体]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[对等体组接收的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP-VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由分配的首选值、是否允许本地]{style="font-family:宋体"}[AS]{lang="EN-US"}[号在接收的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP-VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[AS_PATH]{lang="EN-US"}[属性中出现等。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1848572146}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1957714425}[创建]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址族，并进入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址族视图。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="DA"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1626270441}

[\[Sysname\] bgp 100]{lang="DA"}

[\[Sysname-bgp\] address-family vpnv6]{lang="DA"}

[\[Sysname-bgp-af-vpnv6\]]{lang="DA"}

**IPv6 MPLS L3VPN \-- IPv6 MPLS L3VPN配置命令 \-- disable-dn-bit-check**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[disable-dn-bit-check]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1527185926}[命令用来忽略]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DN]{lang="EN-US"}[位检查。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo disable-dn-bit-check]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1097947148}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1818879541}

[**[disable-dn-bit-check]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1604775974}

[**[undo disable-dn-bit-check]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1141402248}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1527251462}

[[PE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1212896056}[上需要检查]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DN]{lang="EN-US"}[位。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1406647428}

[[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1527054854}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_259256626}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_345257350}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x2138194141}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1288138317}

[[DN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1159968176}[位用于防止路由环路。当]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[设备将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由引入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[，并生成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[时，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[为生成的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[设置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DN]{lang="EN-US"}[位。当其他]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[设备收到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DN]{lang="EN-US"}[位置位的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[后，在计算路由时忽略该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[，从而避免再次通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP]{lang="EN-US"}[协议发布该路由造成路由环路。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[如果]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1527120390}[在路由计算时希望考虑其他]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[发布的所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[，则可以配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[disable-dn-bit-check]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，使得]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[在计算路由时不检查]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DN]{lang="EN-US"}[位，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DN]{lang="EN-US"}[位置位的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[也参与路由计算。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1231309622}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[disable-dn-bit-check]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x718486646}[命令，可能会导致路由环路，请谨慎使用本命令。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[只有在未配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1554889686}**[vpn-instance-capability simple]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令的]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[多实例进程下执行时，本命令才会生效。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1780132787}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1527448070}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[多实例进程]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[不检查]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DN]{lang="EN-US"}[位。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_120104145}

[\[Sysname\] ospfv3 100 vpn-instance vpn1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-ospfv3-100\] disable-dn-bit-check]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_2048235656}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[disable-dn-bit-set]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x458630849}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[display ospfv3]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1461587249}[（三层技术]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[-IP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由命令参考]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[/OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[）]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}
:::

**IPv6 MPLS L3VPN \-- IPv6 MPLS L3VPN配置命令 \-- disable-dn-bit-set**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[disable-dn-bit-set]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_261070626}[命令用来禁止设置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DN]{lang="EN-US"}[位。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo disable-dn-bit-set]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1527316998}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1393245875}

[**[disable-dn-bit-set]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_2087827137}

[**[undo disable-dn-bit-set]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_850184125}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1434930408}

[[PE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1527382534}[上将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由引入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[，并生成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[时，为生成的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[设置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DN]{lang="EN-US"}[位。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x600600290}

[[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1356009044}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1534131501}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_888840288}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1526661638}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x441501228}

[[DN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1794800184}[位用于防止路由环路。当]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[设备将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由引入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[，并生成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[时，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[为生成的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[设置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DN]{lang="EN-US"}[位。当其他]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[设备收到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DN]{lang="EN-US"}[位置位的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[后，在计算路由时忽略该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[，从而避免再次通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP]{lang="EN-US"}[协议发布该路由造成路由环路。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[如果]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1422982941}[希望其他的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[在路由计算时考虑本]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[发布的所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[，则可以执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[disable-dn-bit-set]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，使得本]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[在发布]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[时不设置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DN]{lang="EN-US"}[位。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1462353743}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[disable-dn-bit-set]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1526727174}[命令，可能会导致路由环路]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[，请谨慎使用本命令]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[只有在未配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1223984977}**[vpn-instance-capability simple]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令的]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[多实例进程下执行时，本命令才会生效]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1834327984}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1527185927}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[多实例进程]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[不设置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DN]{lang="EN-US"}[位。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1630936207}

[\[Sysname\] ospfv3 100 vpn-instance vpn1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-ospfv3-100\] disable-dn-bit-set]{lang="EN-US"}

**IPv6 MPLS L3VPN \-- IPv6 MPLS L3VPN配置命令 \-- display bgp routing-table vpnv6**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[display bgp routing-table vpnv6]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x2097510466}[命令用来显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x2000023093}

[**[display bgp routing-table vpnv6 ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ \[ **route-distinguisher** *route-distinguisher* \] \[ *network-address prefix-length* \[ **advertise-info** \] \| **as-path-acl** *as-path-acl-number* \| **community-list** { { *basic-community-list-number* \| *comm-list-name* } \[ **whole-match** \] \| *adv-community-list-number* } \] \| **peer** *ip-address* { **advertised-routes** \| **received-routes** } \[ *network-address* *prefix-length* \| **statistics** \] \| **statistics** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_504967144}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1269389257}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x225227652}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1626270444}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_451897508}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1269058721}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1857400566}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x826530677}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1620246844}

[**[route-distinguisher]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *route-distinguisher*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_355748591}[：显示指定路由标识符的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[route-distinguisher]{lang="EN-US"}*[为路由标识符，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[21]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串。路由标识符有三种格式：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[16]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x303311348}[位自治系统号]{style="font-family:宋体"}[:32]{lang="EN-US"}[位用户自定义数，例如：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[101:3]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[32]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1626729196}[位]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址]{style="font-family:宋体"}[:16]{lang="EN-US"}[位用户自定义数，例如：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[192.168.122.15:1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[32]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1462301110}[位自治系统号：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[16]{lang="EN-US"}[位用户自定义数字，其中的自治系统号最小值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65536]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[例如：]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[65536:1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[network-address prefix-l]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x637548405}*[ength]{lang="EN-US"}*[：显示与指定目的网段地址及前缀长度精确匹配的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[network-address]{lang="EN-US"}*[为目的网段的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址；]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[prefix-l]{lang="EN-US"}[ength]{lang="EN-US"}*[为目的网段地址的前缀长度，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[128]{lang="EN-US"}[。如果没有指定本参数，则显示所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[advertise-info]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1637834167}[：显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由的通告信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[as-path-acl]{lang="EN-US"}***[ as-path-acl-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1637637559}[：显示匹配指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[AS]{lang="EN-US"}[路径过滤列表的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[as-path-acl-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[AS]{lang="EN-US"}[路径过滤列表号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[256]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[communit-list]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1062881532}[：显示匹配指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP]{lang="EN-US"}[团体列表的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[basic-community-list-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1637703095}[：基本团体列表号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[99]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[comm-list-name]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1638030775}[：团体属性列表名，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[63]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[whole-match]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1638096311}[：精确匹配。如果指定了本参数，则只有路由的团体属性列表与指定的团体属性列表完全相同时，才显示该路由的信息；如果未指定本参数，则只要路由的团体属性列表中包含指定的团体属性列表，就显示该路由的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[adv-community-list-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1637506488}[：高级团体列表号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[199]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[peer]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1637572024}[：显示向指定对等体发布或者从指定对等体收到的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[ip-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1637375416}[：对等体的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4]{lang="EN-US"}[地址。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[advertised-routes]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_121949749}[：显示向指定的对等体发布的路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[received-routes]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1637440952}[：显示从指定的对等体接收到的路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[statistics]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1637768632}[：显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由的统计信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1235715915}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1318768052}[显示所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**IPv6 MPLS L3VPN \-- IPv6 MPLS L3VPN配置命令 \-- display ospfv3 sham-link**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[display ospfv3 sham-link]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_523323136}[命令用来显示]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[伪连接信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_233672632}

[**[display ospfv3 ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ *process-id* \] \[ **area** *area-id* \] **sham-link** \[ **verbose** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1527513601}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1661243675}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_685203418}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_971126056}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1527316993}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_183392294}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x682951378}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1527382529}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_158980133}

[*[process-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_173402887}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[进程内的伪连接信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[process-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[进程号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[。如果不指定本参数，则显示所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[进程的伪连接信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[area]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *area-id*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x806831623}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[区域内的伪连接信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[area-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[区域号，可以是整数形式，也可以是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4]{lang="EN-US"}[地址形式。当是整数形式时，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4294967295]{lang="EN-US"}[。如果不指定本参数，则显示所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[区域的伪连接信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[verbose]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1526661633}[：显示伪连接的详细信息。如果不指定本参数，则显示伪连接的概要信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1201016115}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1131142519}[显示所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[伪连接的概要信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**IPv6 MPLS L3VPN \-- IPv6 MPLS L3VPN配置命令 \-- domain-id**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[domain-id]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930404917}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[域标识符。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo domain-id]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1410391522}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x701067089}

[**[domain-id ]{lang="EN-US"}**[{]{lang="EN-US"}[ *domain-id* \[ **secondary** \] \| **null** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x822456762}

[**[undo domain-id ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[]{lang="EN-US"}[ *domain-id* \| **null** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930732597}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1020831017}

[[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x23181351}[域标识符为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_742021041}

[[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930798133}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_628828921}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_942352751}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930601525}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1047935204}

[*[domain-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_84929297}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[域标识符，可以采用以下三种形式：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[整数形式，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_2040475725}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4294967295]{lang="EN-US"}[，例如：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[点分十进制形式，例如：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930667061}[0.0.0.1]{lang="EN-US"}[；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[点分十进制]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x697100765}[:16]{lang="EN-US"}[位二进制数，例如：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0.0.0.1:512]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_985867679}[：指定配置的域标识符作为从域标识符。如果不指定本参数，表示配置的域标识符为主域标识符。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[null]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x213678388}[：指定无域标识符，即团体属性中不携带域标识符。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1929946165}

[[如果通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[domain-id]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1626077647}[命令配置了]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[域标识符，则]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[路由引入到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP]{lang="EN-US"}[时，配置的主域标识符被附加到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由上作为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP]{lang="EN-US"}[的扩展团体属性传递给对端]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[。对端]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[接收到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由后，将本地配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[主域标识符、从域标识符和路由中携带的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[域标识符进行比较：如果主域标识符或从域标识符与路由携带的域标识符相同，且为区域内或区域间路由，则将路由重新引入到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[时，该路由将作为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Inter-Area-Prefix LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[（即]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Type-3 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[）向外发布；否则，该路由将作为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[AS External LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[（即]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Type-5 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[）或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[NSSA External LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[（即]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Type-7 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[）向外发布。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x2023637176}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果一端]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930011701}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[的域标识符配置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[null]{lang="EN-US"}**[，另一端]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[配置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[，则比较域标识符时，认为二者的域标识符相同。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[主域标识符的值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1705345433}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[时，不能配置从域标识符。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **domain-id**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1651862288}[命令时，如果不指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[任何]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[参数，将删除所有域标识符。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[只有在未配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x218350886}**[vpn-instance-capability simple]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令的]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[多实例进程下执行时，本命令才会生效。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930470454}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1683567549}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[多实例进程]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[的主域标识符为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1.1.1.1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[ext-community-type]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_383838840}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[扩展团体属性的类型编码。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[route-type ]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x130685966}*[type-code2]{lang="FR"}*[：指定]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="FR"}[扩展团体属性]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Route Type]{lang="FR"}[的类型编码。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[type-code2]{lang="FR"}*[取值可以为十六进制数值]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0306]{lang="FR"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[8000]{lang="FR"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[router-id]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930601526}*[ type-code3]{lang="FR"}*[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="FR"}[扩展团体属性]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Router ID]{lang="FR"}[的类型编码。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[type-code3]{lang="FR"}*[取值可以为十六进制数值]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0107]{lang="FR"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[8001]{lang="FR"}[。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

**IPv6 MPLS L3VPN \-- IPv6 MPLS L3VPN配置命令 \-- policy vpn-target**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[policy vpn-target]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_735962327}[命令用来对接收到的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN-Target]{lang="EN-US"}[过滤功能，即只将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Export Route Target]{lang="EN-US"}[属性与本地]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Import Route Target]{lang="EN-US"}[属性匹配的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由加入到路由表。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo policy vpn-target]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1490047855}[命令用来取消对]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN-Target]{lang="EN-US"}[过滤功能，即接收所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1772246048}

[**[policy vpn-target]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1273368338}

[**[undo policy vpn-target]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_2029172328}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_549654578}

[[对接收到的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1671453225}[路由使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN-Target]{lang="EN-US"}[过滤功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1719097315}

[[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1993379777}[地址族视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x88025478}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1723143852}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1939837332}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1400485443}

[[在跨域]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN OptionB]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x859870615}[组网中，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ASBR]{lang="EN-US"}[需要保存所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由信息，以通告给对端]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ASBR]{lang="EN-US"}[。这种情况下，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ASBR]{lang="EN-US"}[上需执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo policy vpn-target]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令接收所有的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由信息，不对它们进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Route Target]{lang="EN-US"}[过滤。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1807289588}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_415335003}[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址族视图下，取消对]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Route Target]{lang="EN-US"}[过滤功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[\[Sysname\] bgp 100]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-bgp\] address-family vpnv6]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-bgp-af-vpnv6\] undo policy vpn-target]{lang="EN-US"}

**IPv6 MPLS L3VPN \-- IPv6 MPLS L3VPN配置命令 \-- route-tag**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[route-tag]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1302060906}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[引入路由的外部路由标记值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo route-tag]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930470447}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_2086786540}

[**[route-tag]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *tag-value*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930535983}

[**[undo route-tag]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_274827367}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_54116980}

[[若]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930339375}[骨干网上配置了]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由协议，并且]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[AS]{lang="EN-US"}[号不大于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[，则外部路由标记值的前面两个字节固定为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0xD000]{lang="EN-US"}[，后面的两个字节为本端]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[AS]{lang="EN-US"}[号；否则，外部路由标记值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[。例如，本端]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP AS]{lang="EN-US"}[号为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[时，外部路由标记的缺省值为十进制值]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3489661028]{lang="EN-US"}[，即]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0xD0000000]{lang="EN-US"}[对应的十进制值（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3489660928]{lang="EN-US"}[）]{style="font-family:宋体"}[+100]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1087456275}

[[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_71262241}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930404911}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_603822468}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1602476816}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x167018882}

[*[tag-value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930732591}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[引入路由的外部路由标记值，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4294967295]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_2111336865}

[[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930798127}[双归属（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CE]{lang="EN-US"}[连接两个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[）、且]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CE]{lang="EN-US"}[之间采用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[协议交互私网路由的组网中，外部路由标记可以用来避免路由环路。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CE]{lang="EN-US"}[连接的一台]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[将从对端]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[接收到的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由引入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[，并通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Type-5]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Type-7 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[发布给]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CE]{lang="EN-US"}[时，为该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Type-5]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Type-7 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[添加本地配置的外部路由标记。另一台]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[上如果通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[route-tag-check enable]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令使能了]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[tag]{lang="EN-US"}[标记检查，则]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[接收到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CE]{lang="EN-US"}[发布的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Type-5]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Type-7 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[后，将其中的外部路由标记值与本地配置的值进行比较。如果相同，则在进行路由计算时忽略该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[，从而避免路由环路。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[外部路由标记值的配置方式及各种方式的优先级为：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1696704371}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[import-route]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1366141351}[命令配置的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[tag]{lang="EN-US"}[值优先级最高]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[route-tag]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930601519}[、]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[default tag]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令配置的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[tag]{lang="EN-US"}[优先级其次。其中，]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[route-tag]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令用于]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[设备，]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[default tag]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令用于]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[CE]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[MCE]{lang="EN-US"}[设备。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_921367728}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[同一个区域的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930667055}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[建议配置相同的外部路由标记值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[外部路由标记值不会在]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1628432527}[BGP]{lang="EN-US"}[的扩展团体属性中传递，它只在收到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由并且产生]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3 Type-5]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Type-7 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[路由器上起作用。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[可以为不同的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1409999725}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[进程配置相同的外部路由标记值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[本命令只能在]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1929946159}[多实例进程下执行，并且只有在未配置]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[vpn-instance-capability simple]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[多实例进程下执行时，本命令才会生效。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x343487429}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x422393271}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[多实例进程]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[引入路由的外部路由标记值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930011695}

[\[Sysname\] ospfv3 100 vpn-instance vpn1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-ospfv3-100\] route-tag 100]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x620712146}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[default tag]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x2051937612}[（三层技术]{style="font-family:宋体"}[-IP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由命令参考]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[display ospfv3]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x2113856864}[（三层技术]{style="font-family:宋体"}[-IP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由命令参考]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[import-route]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930470448}[（三层技术]{style="font-family:宋体"}[-IP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由命令参考]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[route-tag-check enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x642096815}
:::

**IPv6 MPLS L3VPN \-- IPv6 MPLS L3VPN配置命令 \-- route-tag-check enable**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[route-tag-check enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930535984}[命令用来使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[的外部路由标记检查。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo route-tag-check enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930339376}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930404912}

[**[route-tag-check enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1007106995}

[**[undo route-tag-check enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1395592442}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930732592}

[[不检查]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x617546490}[的外部路由标记。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930798128}

[[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1743758538}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1143696589}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930601520}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1451219731}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1615271374}

[[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1929946160}[双归属（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CE]{lang="EN-US"}[连接两个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[）、且]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CE]{lang="EN-US"}[之间采用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[协议交互私网路由的组网中，外部路由标记可以用来避免路由环路。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CE]{lang="EN-US"}[连接的一台]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[将从对端]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[接收到的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由引入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[，并通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Type-5]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Type-7 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[发布给]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CE]{lang="EN-US"}[时，为该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Type-5]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Type-7 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[添加本地配置的外部路由标记。另一台]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[上如果通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[route-tag-check enable]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令使能了]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[tag]{lang="EN-US"}[标记检查，则]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[接收到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CE]{lang="EN-US"}[发布的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Type-5]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Type-7 LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[后，将其中的外部路由标记值与本地配置的值进行比较。如果相同，则在进行路由计算时忽略该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[，从而避免路由环路。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[通常情况下，在上述组网中]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_866562760}[通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DN]{lang="EN-US"}[位来避免路由环路。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[的外部路由标记检查只是为了兼容不支持]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DN]{lang="EN-US"}[位的设备。如果网络中不存在这类设备，建议不要配置本命令。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[只有在未配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[vpn-instance-capability simple]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1930011696}[命令的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[多实例进程下执行时，本命令才会生效。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1023996673}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1039230928}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[多实例进程]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[检查]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[tag]{lang="EN-US"}[标记。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x364386510}

[\[Sysname\] ospfv3 100 vpn-instance vpn1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-ospfv3-100\] route-tag-check enable]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1070062726}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[display ospfv3]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x2042094017}[（三层技术]{style="font-family:宋体"}[-IP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由命令参考]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**IPv6 MPLS L3VPN \-- IPv6 MPLS L3VPN配置命令 \-- rr-filter**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[rr-filter]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1668130536}[命令用来创建路由反射器的反射策略：通过配置路由反射器支持的扩展团体属性号，对接收的路由信息进行过滤，只有接收的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPNv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由包含指定的扩展团体属性号时，路由反射器才会反射该路由。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **rr-filter**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1157452717}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1202731862}

[**[rr-filter ]{lang="EN-US"}***[extended-community-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1992855489}

[**[undo rr-filter]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x653741250}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_2009824787}

宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[199]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[当一个集群中存在多个路由反射器时，通过在不同的路由反射器上配置不同的反射策略，可以实现路由反射器之间的负载分担。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x357031058}

**IPv6 MPLS L3VPN \-- IPv6 MPLS L3VPN配置命令 \-- sham-link**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[sham-link]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x404676374}[命令用来创建一条]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[伪连接。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo sham-link]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x364648654}[命令用来删除一条已有的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[伪连接，或者将指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[伪连接的参数恢复为缺省值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1174897487}

[**[sham-link]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *source-ipv6-address destination-ipv6-address* \[ **cost** *cost* \| **dead** *dead-interval* \| **hello** *hello-interval* \| **instance** *instance-id* \| **ipsec-profile** *profile-name* \| **retransmit** *retrans-interval* \| **trans-delay** *delay* \] \*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x364714190}

[**[undo sham-link]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *source-ipv6-address destination-ipv6-address* \[ **cost** \| **dead** \| **hello** \| **ipsec-profile** \| **retransmit** \| **trans-delay** \] \*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x846466084}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_984110516}

[[设备上不存在任何]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x364517582}[伪连接。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x485207702}

[[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x364583118}[区域视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_709197559}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_576167386}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x363862222}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_91491072}

[*[source-ipv6-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_289336827}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[伪连接的源]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[destination-ipv6-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x363927758}[：]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[伪连接的目的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[cost]{lang="EN-US"}***[ cost]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x730967581}[：伪连接开销，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[，缺省值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[dead ]{lang="EN-US"}***[dead-interval]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x364386511}[：失效时间间隔，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[32768]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒，缺省值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[hello-interval]{lang="EN-US"}*[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4]{lang="EN-US"}[倍。建立伪连接的两个路由器上需要配置相同的]{style="font-family:
宋体"}*[dead-interval]{lang="EN-US"}*[，且至少为]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[hello-interval]{lang="EN-US"}*[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4]{lang="EN-US"}[倍。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[**[hello]{lang="EN-US"}***[ hello-interval]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1070128262}[：接口上发送]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Hello]{lang="EN-US"}[报文的时间间隔，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[8192]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒，缺省值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。建立伪连接的两个路由器上需要配置相同的]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[hello-interval]{lang="EN-US"}*[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[instance]{lang="EN-US"}***[ instance-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x831221742}[：伪连接的实例]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ID]{lang="EN-US"}[，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[255]{lang="EN-US"}[，缺省值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ipsec-profile]{lang="EN-US"}***[ profile-name]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x364452047}[：指定伪连接引用的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPsec]{lang="EN-US"}[安全框架。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[profile-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPsec]{lang="EN-US"}[安全框架名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[63]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，不区分大小写。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[retransmit]{lang="EN-US"}***[ retrans-interval]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x44234114}[：接口上重传]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[报文的时间间隔，取值范围是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3600]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒，缺省值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[5]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[trans-delay ]{lang="EN-US"}***[delay]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x349731444}[：接口上延迟发送]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSA]{lang="EN-US"}[报文的时间间隔，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3600]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒，缺省值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x364255439}

[[属于同一个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_316499878}[的两个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Site]{lang="EN-US"}[之间存在一条区域内]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[链路（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[backdoor]{lang="EN-US"}[链路）时，为了避免]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[流量总是通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[backdoor]{lang="EN-US"}[链路转发而不走骨干网，可以在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[设备之间建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[伪连接（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[sham-link]{lang="EN-US"}[），使经过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[骨干网的路由也成为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[区域内路由，确保]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[流量通过骨干网转发。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x364320975}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x404610838}[创建一条]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[伪连接，源地址为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1::1]{lang="EN-US"}[，目的地址为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2::2]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x1827278431}

[\[Sysname\] ospfv3 100 vpn-instance vpn1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-ospfv3-100\] area 0]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-ospfv3-100-area-0.0.0.0\] sham-link 1::1 2::2]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x364648655}

**IPv6 MPLS L3VPN \-- IPv6 MPLS L3VPN配置命令 \-- vpn-instance-capability simple**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[vpn-instance-capability simple]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x364714191}[命令用来关闭]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[的路由环路检测功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo vpn-instance-capability]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x846531620}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x364517583}

[**[vpn-instance-capability simple]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x485273238}

[**[undo vpn-instance-capability]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_698077098}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x364583119}

[[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_709263095}[的路由环路检测功能处于开启状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_89538044}

[[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x363862223}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_91556608}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x363927759}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x730902045}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1000786985}

[[MCE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1409551543}[组网环境中，需要在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MCE]{lang="EN-US"}[设备上关闭]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[的路由环路检测功能。否则，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MCE]{lang="EN-US"}[设备不会接收]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[设备发送过来的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[路由，导致路由丢失。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[该命令只能在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x364386512}[多实例进程下执行。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_1070193798}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x364452048}[关闭]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}[多实例进程]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[的路由环路检测功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1771_x9971_x44823938}

[\[Sysname\] ospfv3 100 vpn-instance vpn1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-ospfv3-100\] vpn-instance-capability simple]{lang="EN-US"}

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}
:::
